| Collect Payment Error Codes | UPIPPI | E4391 | Transaction failed due to customer's account being inactive or dormant | Verify final payment status, show a customer-safe retry or alternate-payment message, and use a new txnid for any new payment attempt. |
| Collect Payment Error Codes | UPIPPI | E706 | You do not have sufficient credit limit to complete this transaction. | Verify final payment status, show a customer-safe retry or alternate-payment message, and use a new txnid for any new payment attempt. |
| Collect Payment Error Codes | VERNEGATIVE | Verification failure | Indicates that a verification call confirms the transaction has failed or been declined. | Verify final payment status, show a customer-safe retry or alternate-payment message, and use a new txnid for any new payment attempt. |
